    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

Subsumption Test Results (50 samples, seq_len=2048)\\n\\n### 核心指标\\n- Linearity ratio: 0.264 ± 0.007 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n- Magnitude ratio: 0.158 ± 0.009 (SPON correction / predictor effect)\\n- Cosine similarity: 0.196 ± 0.027 (near-orthogonal)\\n\\n### Effect magnitudes\\n- ‖D(predictor)‖ = 482.65 (KL predictor logit shift)\\n- ‖D(SPON)‖ = 76.20 (SPON bias correction logit shift)\\n- ‖interaction‖ = 147.74 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n\\n### 结论：Informative — SPON corrections are orthogonal and negligibly small\\n\\n1. **SPON 与 mask 效应正交**：cos_sim ≈ 0.20，两种修正作用在不同维度\\n2. **SPON 量级微小**：仅为 predictor 效应的 16%（76 vs 483）\\n3. **逐层精确可加**：交互项（148）来源于跨层级联非线性（down_proj 是线性的，层内 mask+bias 严格可加），非层内交互\\n4. **支持 D010 \\\"marginal complement\\\" framing**：SPON 提供的是正交但微弱的补充，不构成有意义的 compositional gain\\n\\n⚠️ 不使用 \\\"subsumption\\\" 措辞——数据显示正交性而非包含关系。正确表述：SPON corrections are orthogonal to and much smaller than predictor mask effects; the non-zero interaction term arises from cross-layer cascading, not intra-layer coupling.\\n\\n### 产出文件\\nresults/subsumption_test/subsumption_results.json (15KB)\", \"completed_at\": \"2026-05-15T20:29:47.949Z\", \"retry_count\": 0, \"created_at\": \"2026-05-16T00:35:56.904Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T02:58:48.632Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"GSM8K 生成质量对比：dense vs KL sparse 50% greedy 生成 5-10 \"/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/checkpoints/mvp_kl_norm_v2_s42/predictor_kl_normalized.pt\", \"log_path\": \"/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/artifacts/gsm8k_gen_compare.txt\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"completed_at\": \"2026-05-16T00:43:29.657Z\", \"notes\": \"## GSM8K 生成质量对比：Dense vs KL Sparse 50% (10 samples)\\n\\n### 结论：推理能力根本性丧失（模式 A），非仅数字算错\\n\\n| 维度 | Dense | Sparse 50% |\\n|------|-------|------------|\\n| 推理连贯性 | 多步推理，逻辑清晰 | 1-3 句后进入无限重复循环 |\\n| 正确答案 | 多数正确 | 0/10 |\\n| 重复退化 | 无 | 10/10 全部出现 |\\n| 拼写质量 | 正常 | 退化（crabs→crbs, skein→skeen）|\\n\\n### 退化模式\\nSparse 模型能生成与问题相关的开头（如 \\\"First, we need to know...\\\"），但随即进入同一句话的无限重复（15+ 次）。Sample 7 中正确算出 9×2=18，但无法继续推理。\\n\\n### 对论文的意义\\n50% sparsity 摧毁了深层推理能力。PPL-downstream disconnect 的机制：PPL 衡量 next-token 平均质量，repetition loop 的每个重复 token 仍有较高概率（模型\\\"自信地\\\"重复），但推理链断裂。\", \"parent\": \"lm_eval_kl_s42\"}, \"d030_kl40_uniform\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"retry_count\": 0, \"created_at\": \"/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/results/d030_kl50_uniform/\", \"completed_at\": \"2026-05-16T05:09:25.315Z\", \"notes\": \"## D030 50% Uniform 结果\\n\\n50% uniform per-layer allocation 未能修复 downstream collapse。MMLU 0.2777（接近随机 0.25），GSM8K 0.0212，与 global top-k 的 0.2591/0.0121 几乎无差异。\\n\\n### 与 30% Uniform 对比\\n30% uniform 将 MMLU 从 0.2874 拉到 0.4061（+12pp），但 50% uniform 对 MMLU 仅 +1.86pp。说明 uniform allocation 的修复效应在 50% 下完全消失。\\n\\n### 结论\\n50% 是 downstream capability 的断崖点。无论 per-layer allocation 策略如何，50% sparsity 对 SwiGLU FFN 的知识检索和推理能力有根本性破坏。支撑 Narrative A（50% collapse is universal）。\\n\\n### 对论文的意义\\n- Claim 3 需要调整：30% 可以作为\\\"推荐配置\\\"叙述（PPL 8.82, MMLU 0.4061），但 50% 只能用于 PPL 场景\\n- 论文应报告 30% uniform 为 downstream-safe operating point\", \"parent\": \"d030_kl30_uniform\"}, \"account\": \"root\", \"wall_clock_seconds\": 2215.3, \"gpu_hours\": 0.62}, \"notes\": \"TEAL 50% downstream 结果确认 collapse 是 universal：\\n\\n| Task | TEAL 50% | KL 50% | Dense | TEAL drop | KL drop |\\n|------|----------|--------|-------|-----------|---------|\\n| arc_challenge (acc_norm) | 0.2910 | 0.3771 | 0.5819 | -50.0% | -35.2% |\\n| winogrande (acc) | 0.5556 | 0.6156 | 0.7814 | -28.9% | -21.2% |\\n| mmlu (acc) | 0.2856 | 0.2591 | 0.6532 | -56.3% | -60.3% |\\n| gsm8k (flexible-extract) | 0.0190 | 0.0121 | 0.5004 | -96.2% | -97.6% |\\n\\n关键发现：\\n1. TEAL 和 KL 在 MMLU/GSM8K 上均出现灾难性崩溃（接近随机水平），证明 50% sparsity downstream collapse 不是 KL predictor 特有问题\\n2. TEAL 在 ARC/WinoGrande 上甚至比 KL 更差（TEAL drop -50%/-29% vs KL drop -35%/-21%），说明 training-free 方法在简单 task 上也不如 learned predictor\\n3. MMLU/GSM8K 两者均近随机（MMLU ~0.25-0.29 = 4-choice random level），collapse 程度相当\\n4. 结论：50% unstructured sparsity 对 SwiGLU FFN 的 downstream capability 有根本性破坏，与 sparsity 选择方法无关\", \"reproduce\": {
  "task_type": "fix:revkl_30_s42_boolq_uniform",
  "project": "distill_sparse_swiglu",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务\n修复 revkl_30_s42_boolq_uniform 实验的数据集缓存问题并重跑评估。\n\n## 背景\n实验在 westd-41718 上失败，原因是 super_glue 数据集未缓存且无法联网下载。westd-34887 上有缓存。\n\n## 步骤\n\n### 1. 在 westd-41718 上启用代理并下载数据集\nSSH 到 westd-41718，执行：\n```bash\ncd /root/distill_sparse_swiglu\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\nsource /etc/network_turbo\nexport HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\nexport REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\nexport SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\npython -c \"from datasets import load_dataset; ds = load_dataset('super_glue', 'boolq'); print('OK', len(ds['validation']))\"\n```\n\n如果 network_turbo 不存在或下载失败，备选方案：从 westd-34887 rsync 缓存：\n```bash\n# 先在 westd-34887 上确认缓存位置\nls -la /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/datasets/super_glue/\n# 然后用 rsync 或 scp 同步（需要确认两台机器之间能否直连）\n```\n\n### 2. 验证数据集可用\n```bash\nexport HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\nexport HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1\npython -c \"from datasets import load_dataset; ds = load_dataset('super_glue', 'boolq'); print('OFFLINE OK', len(ds['validation']))\"\n```\n\n### 3. 重跑评估（使用 submit_training_job）\n数据集就绪后，用 `submit_training_job` 重新提交实验：\n- exp_id: revkl_30_s42_boolq_uniform\n- server: westd-41718\n- GPU: cuda:1（从 0 开始编号，实际物理 GPU 1）\n- 命令：\n```bash\ncd /root/distill_sparse_swiglu && source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && export HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache && export HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1 TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1 HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 && python -u src/benchmark_eval.py --exp_id revkl_30_s42_boolq_uniform --model_name_or_path /root/autodl-tmp/models/llama-3.1-8b --checkpoint checkpoints/reverse_kl_30_s42/predictor_reverse_kl.pt --sparsity_mode teal_global --sparsity_target 0.3 --allocation uniform --tasks boolq --seq_len 2048 --gpu 0\n```\n\n### 4. 验证实验已启动\n确认进程在跑，检查日志前几行没有报错。\n\n## 重要\n- GPU 编号：脚本内用 --gpu 0（框架自动映射 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES）\n- 使用 submit_training_job 提交，不要用 nohup\n- 如果 cuda:1 被占用，改用其他空闲 GPU（先检查 nvidia-smi）",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh",
    "registry"
  ]
}
